- 2026-08-15 — S88: the first real playthrough of Era 4, and what it actually found. Entered the

  rAF). Found and fixed two real, high-severity bugs: (1) the Close's "Restart as you are." card was
  compositing over the finale's own four-year panels forever, because `E4Offers.ownsField` never expires
  once `stage === 'done'` and `E4Shell.draw()` kept painting them under the update's small bare modal —
  `theme/era4.ts`'s own `_docFinale` forbids exactly this; fixed by having the shell paint a plain
  `ERA4.field` once handed off, reproduced and re-verified live; (2) the ⟲ turn-assist button targeted a
  hardcoded global 0°/180° heading instead of the seat's own forward — confirmed live at Maya's seat
  (270°), the button only turned 90° instead of the ~180° the ball's respite is built on (§16 CLASS 4,
  predicted 2026-08-12, never fixed); made `isBackYaw()`/`doFlip()` seat-relative in `src/engine/app.ts`,
  byte-identical at seatYaw 0 (E1/E2), verified live at Maya's seat (270° → 90°). Measured draw calls at
  the E4 seat: 31 forward, **141 turned** against the ≤75 budget (not re-baselined; down from S79's 177,
  a remeasurement not a fix) — real GPU cost on the exact facing the ball holds a player on for three

  OVERLAP findings room-audit reports are confirmed NOT Era-4-specific (byte-identical in r1–r3 too,

  numbered list, and the fps/draw-call table: `docs/reinterp/08_STATUS_REGISTER.md` §28. tsc/test/build

- 2026-07-06 — Reinterp Session 15 — FURNITURE FIXED (Round 24e; Sérgio's screenshots: "computer floating, bed non-existent, bookcase in the middle of nowhere, models misplaced"). Root cause found: the models rendered but the MORPH (clusterMorph) stomped each prop's transform with its BOX size/pos AFTER the model self-placed → squished/floating furniture. Fixes: (1) MORPH IS NOW MODEL-AWARE — PropHandle.model flag; clusterMorph applyTarget/snapTo/goToState SKIP pos/scale/color for model props and toggle only their presence (enabled), so a model wrapper is never distorted. (2) DETERMINISTIC PLACEMENT — spawnModel puts the mesh in a WRAPPER, shifting the model child by its measured native centre (cx/cz/baseY in the manifest, from tools measurement) so it's centred on the prop's floor pos + rotated cleanly; the wrapper (scale 1) is what the room transforms. Replaces the flaky runtime-AABB approach. (3) NATIVE KENNEY TONES KEPT — removed the flat monochrome recolor (Sérgio: "I don't want multi-tone, only if very subtle"; Kenney's own muted low-poly palette IS the subtle multi-tone) → the bed reads as a bed, the chair has a red cushion, the desk is wood. (4) ROOM 1 BED moved BACK out of the west doorway (r2 delta reposition + shortened) — clears the seated sightline into Room 2 (Sérgio: "the bed can't be there"); a T-layout constraint (the X-layout would free a wall). (5) DEV-SERVER RACE fixed — models preload SEQUENTIALLY + retry-once (vite dev occasionally served index.html for a public GLB under concurrent load → "invalid magic number"); and preload is now gated to reinterp (the shipped `/` baseline uses boxes, so it no longer wastefully loads/faults on the GLBs). VERIFIED in the 3D view: E2 west + east rooms — desk/chair/bed/bookcase/nightstand/rug all correctly placed, native tones, CRT sitting ON the desk (not floating); E4 Maya — same, with the lamp carried onto her desk + dark window + folders/phone; __modelsLoaded shows all 6 loaded (sequential+retry reliable); npm test + build green (GLBs copied to dist/assets/models/); `/` baseline now model-free. S44 staged its two deletions; S43 (which correctly ran `git status` first) staged its own four files and committed without pathspecs, sweeping S44's deletions into a1d92ef — a commit that cannot build in isolation, since app.ts still imports openingBoardDressing there while the module is deleted in the same commit. The ETHICS BOUNDARY is a hard runtime check, not just a comment: the script refuses to render any manifest entry whose `register` isn't `"apparatus"` — synthesize the machine, never the person, never a `felt` survivor-side line. `data/audio/tts_manifest.json` (new) holds the first entry: S2R.5's PureMail apology, read in Lamby's voice — the apparatus reading its own death notice in the cheerful assistant timbre, text sourced from `data/dialog/s2_caleb.json`'s `pureMail` block, PORTED VERBATIM there already. `src/audio/tapeAudio.ts` gained the registry entry (`lamby_puremail_apology.wav`, with a comment noting it's APPARATUS audio so it skips `degrade_audio.sh`'s tape-degradation pass, which is HUMAN/TAPE-only by that script's own doctrine) plus a small `playOnce(name)` export — one-shot playback outside the boombox bus, same missing-file-safe pattern as everything else in that module, for the future read-aloud button (`pureMail.readAloudTrack`, still a hook, still unwired — UI files were outside this session's fence). `npm run tts` added as the local-only invocation alias. **Verified for real:** ran the full pipeline end to end (one retry needed — a `run_in_background` misuse killed the first download mid-flight when the outer shell exited after backgrounding python a second time internally; the second attempt hit a `huggingface_hub` tmp-dir race on a redundant re-download check and needed a bare retry once the cache was already populated — neither was this tool's bug). The committed `public/assets/audio/lamby_puremail_apology.wav` is a real 30.8s 16-bit/44.1kHz mono WAV; loaded it through the actual dev server and decoded it with the browser's own WebAudio API (`decodeAudioData`, 30.811s, byte-exact), then imported `tapeAudio.ts` live in-page and called `isAudioAvailable`/`playOnce` directly — the real clip played (audible `currentTime` advancing) and a fabricated missing name returned `null` with zero console errors. `npm test` + `npx tsc --noEmit` green throughout. Extends to: (a) the read-aloud accessibility layer for long in-world text (same registry + `playOnce`, next session wires the button), (b) a future full Lambient speaking voice (same manifest, new entries, same ethics gate). - 2026-07-25 — Lamby concept REWRITTEN + S54 specced (Opus). (1) PART 1 REDESIGNED — Sérgio rejected my first version outright: I had built it on the Witness side using the provotype grammar, and he wants the opposite ("it doesn't need to be about the Witness side… Lamby should be a ridiculous game, that helps understand that Update-Available is about the DIGITAL aspects of SOGICE… a fun way to explore game styles and genres"). Rewritten as **JUST CHANGE**, a standalone arcade game on its own GitHub Pages site running the full 4:45 song, whose organising idea is that THE GENRE UPDATES WITH EACH ERA and that progression IS the argument: 1997 floppy maze/Snake (chunky, unfair, a half-drawn mascot) → 2003 Flash whack-a-mole with a streak counter (Lamby now polished and congratulating you) → 2016 mobile endless-runner with gacha/IAP ("three easy payments of yourself", a daily login streak you didn't ask for) → NOW, ambient AI, where THE GAME PLAYS ITSELF: your inputs stop mattering, Lamby autocompletes your moves, you get a perfect score you didn't earn. Stage 4 is both the punchline and the thesis — the endpoint of the digital apparatus is that it no longer needs your participation, which lands simultaneously as a game-design joke (an idle game! a game with no player!) and as the collapse the tone law requires; the satire sits in the mascot's self-presentation throughout and curdles there. The song drives the structure, with "I'm always watching you" arriving exactly as the automation takes over. Per his point 4, STAGE 1 ALSO LIVES INSIDE THE WEBXR as a period-correct floppy game on the Era-1 machine — the same object in two homes thirty years apart, the strongest link between piece and promo, and nearly free since E1's desktop already runs canvas apps. Laws carried: satire never targets the player or the steered figure; no real orgs/people/testimony except on the statused end card; no storage/network/analytics (it must not do the thing it depicts); NO WIN STATE THAT ENDORSES THE APPARATUS — "winning" stage 4 is the indictment. (2) PART 2 DEVELOPED with the narrative positioning he asked for. Recommendation: E1 AS PRECURSOR, because per the R28 amendment Lamby the CHARACTER doesn't exist in Era 1 — so finding lamby_rig.exe on a 1997 machine means you meet the rigging tool BEFORE the puppet, as an unfinished dev artifact left on a disk. It pays off twice later: you recognise Lamby at his E2 debut knowing he was built, and at S2R.3C's shame beat YOU HAVE ALREADY MOVED THE SAD SLIDER YOURSELF an era earlier, out of curiosity — nothing else in the piece can poison a beat retroactively like that. It also solves the tone risk (in E1 the tool isn't charming yet, so there's no charm to break the grave register with — the charm arrives later, and now you know where charm comes from) and the anachronism is itself the content: the digital apparatus was being built before it was deployed. Offered as an ambitious variant: THE RIG AGES WITH HIM across all four eras (crude dev tool → polished → the character has moved to the cloud and the local rig controls nothing → gone), turning a one-off find into a longitudinal thread about who holds the pen. Rules: operable register, never during felt, finding it is NEVER rewarded (the frame never plays), not a minigame, filed but never remarked on. Song rights confirmed clear by Sérgio. (3) S54 SPECCED from his screenshot — S52 fixed the boombox's FACING but did it by standing the unit on its end, so it now reads as a tower/vending machine rather than a landscape boombox; the spec is to lay it down horizontally on its base using S52's existing tilt mechanism, then RE-MEASURE THE DOCKED-TAPE SPOT (S52 learned this the hard way — moving the boombox put the docked tape inside its solid body). Flagged a real conflict rather than papering over it: his "drop 45 degrees" describes the look he wants, but CLAUDE.md's pixel discipline says 90°-step rotations only, and from the screenshot laying it flat is a 90° move anyway — the session is told to build toward the GOAL, verify by eye from the seat pose, and if a non-90° value is genuinely needed, declare it in the log as a deliberate deviation for Sérgio to accept or reject. The tapes get the real work: they currently float as three small flat rectangles that read as debris, so they must rest on the measured shelf, get BIGGER, and be arranged so they read as a set you choose from (standing on edge in a rack, or leaning on the player) — with the acceptance test written as "would a first-time player at the seat understand these are things they can touch?" rather than anything aesthetic. Also folded in: teddyBox's likely shelf-height bug (same class as cdStack, and the session is already measuring that shelf) and a warning that click geometry has now been invalidated by prop moves three sessions running. Housekeeping logged: palette baseline can tighten 44→42 after S53, and S51's two follow-ups in app.ts/os.ts — netvisionWasPlaying never resets, and the netvisionBreak debug jump seeks duration−3 which at 114s now lands at 111s, deep in the tear instead of at the break's start (that one matters, it's how the break gets reviewed). npm test green; no code touched.

(1) JUST_CHANGE_BUILD_SPEC_2026-07-25.md written — Sérgio greenlit Part 1 ("truly like it"), own repo + GitHub Pages, song rights cleared. Four stages on one continuous 4:45 Treblo track, transitions landing on song section boundaries, with "I'm always watching you" timed to arrive exactly as Stage 4's automation takes over: S1 1997 THE UN-WALK (floppy maze, four colours, deliberately unfair, a crude half-drawn Lamby — name is parent canon from s1_kit.json's "TriedPath Un-Walk"); S2 2003 DAILY REALIGNMENT (Flash whack-a-mole, XP chrome, a streak counter reusing the piece's 412 that resets to zero on one miss — the joke being that the number is the apparatus's, not yours); S3 2016 GRACE QUEUE (mobile endless-runner + gacha, "three easy payments of yourself" from the infomercial, an unrequested daily-login streak); S4 NOW LAMBY KNOWS (the game plays itself — inputs stop mattering, Lamby first assists then anticipates then simply plays, the score climbs perfectly without you). Stage 4 is where the mascot's charm curdles, satisfying the collapse law, and lands as a game-design joke and the thesis at once: a game with no player, the apparatus's endpoint being that it will be well on your behalf without requiring consent. Hard laws written into the prompt: no storage/network/analytics (it must not do the thing it depicts, and the end card says nothing was kept), invented marks only with real material confined to a statused end card, NO WIN STATE THAT ENDORSES THE APPARATUS (Stage 4's perfect score is the indictment), content note first, Leave from frame one, satire never landing on the steered figure — with an explicit instruction to STOP and flag rather than ship if a joke would land on a queer person instead of the system. Per his confirmed point 4, Stage 1 must be authored as a self-contained liftable module so it ALSO ships inside the WebXR as a period floppy game on the Era-1 machine — one object in two homes thirty years apart. Reuses lambyChar.ts (vendored, sync point documented) so both Lambys stay one Lamby. (2) S55 SPECCED — the E1 lamby_rig.exe easter egg, placement adopted as recommended. Tone requirement made load-bearing: in E1 it must read as an UNFINISHED DEV ARTIFACT (placeholder labels, a half-drawn lamb that animates poorly) because E1's register is grave and a polished playful rig lab would break it — the charm arrives in E2, and by then the player knows where charm comes from. Deliberately the same crude Lamby as Just Change's Stage 1. Never rewarded, never acknowledged, never reachable during a felt scene, filed silently. SCOPE HELD to E1 only per Sérgio's point 4 — he wants other narrative elements fixed before expanding the egg across all eras, so the four-era "rig ages with him" variant is DEFERRED not closed, and the prompt forbids scaffolding for it (no speculative generality). Its beat must be added to the debug panel or C6 fails, which is the guard working as designed. (3) TRANSPARENCY UNBLOCKED — S53 built the panel's transparency and verified legibility (contrast 14.8→14.7:1 body, 4.08→4.04:1 faintest) but it reveals nothing because main.ts starts the engine inside the panel's own continue callback; S53 correctly stopped and called it an ethics question rather than a build one. Sérgio ruled: "I would assume that us seeing a bit of the space is not the start of the experience" → approved, a dark still moonlit room behind a legible note is not the experience beginning. Recorded what a session must now do, including the non-obvious constraint: HOLD THE DESCENT until enter is pressed, because the arc must not play out unseen behind the panel — the entrance is the response to logging in and spending it behind the note would waste the gesture. Also noted he hasn't seen screenshots of this and should, since the transparency was judged against an injected stand-in rather than the real room. - 2026-07-25 — Experience priority assessed; NEXT UP rebuilt (Opus). Sérgio deprioritised Just Change ("let's focus on changes to the overall experience"), so I assessed the spine against the CODE rather than the plan. Finding, and it dominates everything else on the queue: ERA 2'S CLIMAX LEADS NOWHERE. spine.ts is sound — it drives all four transitions on real gates (u2 after T1_DELAY; u3 on sendResolved('s2') + UPDATE_GAP; u4 on sendResolved('s4'); close after E4_HOLD) and sends are offered diegetically via spine.ts:61 → os.offerSend, so the chain is not debug-only. But os.ts's `onThreadDone` — the callback that fires after the residue line, the era's emotional climax — does nothing except null the thread and mark dirty. So after "Then it was never me that was broken…" the player is returned to a desktop to WAIT FOR A SEND TO RESOLVE AND A TIMER TO ELAPSE. The most important moment in Era 2 is narratively inert: it changes nothing, arms nothing, leads nowhere. os.ts:361 says so in its own comment (S45 was correctly scoped; the S2R.7 lane simply never ran). This is the single highest-value change available — everything else on the queue ADDS material, this makes the material that already exists LAND. Wrote REINTERP_EXPERIENCE_PRIORITY_2026-07-25.md with the spine table, the break, and the S57 prompt: the u3 ritual in the established grammar (notification with "Remind me later" working once → EULA → changelog-as-thesis + glitch → restart, reusing u2's machinery not a parallel one), the belongings beat firing a SECOND time in a different life (the E1 pass was a teenager's room, this one an adult's), the restart delivering the player to Room 2 where Vera's era begins as Daniel's ends, and `subject migrated — file retained` as the last filing under his name. Deliberately did NOT prescribe how the residue connects to the ending — told the session to read spine.ts first and work WITH its existing gate rather than forcing u3 the instant the residue lands, and to ARGUE its choice in the log, because "the ending should feel caused, not merely timed" is a dramaturgy call and not mine to settle. D31 confirmed STILL OPEN (checklist line 418, "let's think about this", proposed 2026-07-12, unstruck for 13 days): the dispersal is the era's closing image AND the piece's stated reason the rooms open, so S57 is instructed to check the checklist and build items 1-5 with a clean seam if it is still unstruck rather than improvise it. Surfaced it to Sérgio with my read attached but not assumed — "the program died; the watching dispersed" is the whole thesis in six words and it EARNS the rooms opening rather than announcing it, so I would build it. Also rebuilt NEXT UP: items 1-3 (S40/S41/S42) had all shipped and the list had gone stale exactly the way R29 diagnosed and fixed once already — worth noting that the rot recurs whenever sessions append to DONE without pruning NEXT UP, which is a process gap the STATUS headers and C5/C6 don't cover because it is prose, not structure. - 2026-07-25 — D31 checked against GPAHE at Sérgio's suggestion, and it settles it (Opus). He asked whether the three GPAHE reports (conversion therapy online: the players / the ecosystem / the ecosystem in 2023) help decide D31, the "LAMBY NEVER LEAVES" dispersal staging he flagged "let's think about this" on 2026-07-12 and left unstruck for 13 days. They do, decisively, and in the direction of adopting it: THE BEAT IS DOCUMENTED HISTORY, NOT A DRAMATIC INVENTION. GPAHE's "The Players" records that after Exodus International's 2013 shutdown the movement survived through rebranding ("rainbow-washing", softer messaging, new names), DECENTRALIZATION ("from centralized structures to networks of independent affiliates"), diversification into anti-trans services, and younger public faces; "The Ecosystem" finds the digital form "decentralizes authority — individual practitioners and small nonprofits reach global audiences without traditional institutional oversight, while algorithmic amplification creates pathways that older organizational structures couldn't match." Critically, the project's OWN knowledge base already carries this (docs/ChatGPT analysis.md, GPAHE-cited: "the rhetoric did not disappear. It migrated into platform-native forms") — so D31 was never speculative, only un-cited. Recommendation logged: STRIKE — adopt, and the beat can carry status: documentary / confidence high. THREE THINGS THE EVIDENCE ADDS. (1) A refinement that may improve the staging: GPAHE's documented mechanism is RENAMING, not just scattering — NARTH became ATCSI in 2014, and the 2023 report finds providers adopting medical-sounding language ("reintegrative therapy", "SAFE-T") specifically to evade content moderation — so "could not be removed — RENAMED" may beat "migrating", it is better sourced, more frightening, and it rhymes with the piece's own update-as-survival grammar and with D5's "change"→"calling" softening. (2) A caution: "Exodus shut down" is only half true — GPAHE lists Exodus Global Alliance as currently operating 200+ affiliated ministries worldwide, so Exodus INTERNATIONAL (US) closed in 2013 while a differently-named sibling never stopped; that gives the u3 "migrated to a brighter place" line a real referent, but it is a real, named, currently-operating organisation and under CLAUDE.md may appear in dossier/provenance ONLY, never in the fiction, which keeps its invented marks. (3) An ethics correction to the framing: the 2023 report finds deplatforming PARTLY WORKED (Facebook and Instagram eliminated the recommendation rabbit holes, Bing improved markedly, TikTok performs best and shows warnings; YouTube remains "a disaster" and non-English content is least moderated) — so the dispersal must be staged as a system that survived by scattering, NOT as omnipotence, which matters for the piece's own laws (the respite is never a trap; the Close is solidarity, not defeat). Also noted: updates.json ALREADY says "Your file has been migrated to a brighter place" (u3) and "your journey continues where you already are: everywhere" (u4) — the copy has been writing toward D31 for two weeks without it being struck, which suggests the instinct preceded the evidence. Consequences recorded: if struck, S57 builds item 6 rather than leaving a seam; the beat becomes a citable Close-constellation node with status documentary; and it answers checklist §D's u3 grounding row, which currently has no data/ source at all. I think we need that documented spine. And it does help with the D31 of changing the way the rooms open up." Three things landed. (1) D31 struck/adopted in the checklist WITH the GPAHE refinements: the uninstaller line becomes "could not be removed — RENAMED" rather than "migrating", because GPAHE's documented mechanism is renaming (NARTH→ATCSI 2014; providers relabelling in medical-sounding language — "reintegrative therapy", "SAFE-T" — specifically to evade content moderation), which is both better sourced and a rhyme with the piece's own update-as-survival grammar; the beat carries status: documentary; real organisations stay dossier-only so the fiction keeps Restorify→GracePlatform; and it is staged as SURVIVAL BY SCATTERING never omnipotence, since GPAHE 2023 found deplatforming partly worked (Facebook/Instagram killed the rabbit holes, Bing improved, TikTok shows warnings; YouTube remains "a disaster", non-English content least moderated) and an all-powerful apparatus would be both inaccurate and against the piece's own laws — the respite is never a trap, the Close is solidarity not defeat. (2) THE INFRASTRUCTURE SPINE adopted as the documented backbone — REINTERP_INFRASTRUCTURE_SPINE_2026-07-25.md. The upgrade Sérgio identified: the rooms no longer open because the apparatus multiplied, they open because it STOPPED BEING A PLACE YOU GO TO and moved into the infrastructure already in use (GPAHE: Google/Bing ~95% of search traffic, YouTube monetising provider video, Facebook/Instagram, PayPal and Amazon Smile donations, Alexa voice results — ordinary services carrying it, not a network of ideological sites). His insight recorded as the load-bearing part: this doesn't merely justify the dispersal, it changes what the room-opening MEANS — the walls don't come down because more rooms became available, they come down because walls stopped meaning anything, the thing you could once close a door on now being in the room's ordinary furniture. That gives a documented four-era spine where each era is the apparatus occupying a different KIND of thing: E1 a PLACE (residential program, mailed kit, tape) → E2 a PROGRAM you open (Restorify) → E3 a PLATFORM you live inside (GraceQueue/SisterSignal) → E4 AMBIENT, not a service but IN the services. E2→E3 is exactly where it stops being something you open and becomes something you are inside, so the spatial law and the documented history now say the same thing. (3) The ethics constraint this creates, made binding: GPAHE names real companies, and under CLAUDE.md they may appear in dossier/provenance ONLY, never in a room — with a good design consequence, that E4's surfaces should not be conversion services but ORDINARY services that happen to carry it (a wellness app, a search field, a voice assistant, a payments screen, all invented, all mundane), because the horror in E4 is not that the apparatus got louder but that it became indistinguishable from utilities. That is a sharper brief for E4 than "ambient care" alone and it now comes with citations. S57's item 6 flipped from "build only if D31 is struck" to BUILD IT, carrying the three constraints inline, and the priority doc's stale "one decision is owed" section rewritten to record the strike. Two questions left open for Sérgio's voice pass rather than decided by default: how explicit E4 gets about naming the KINDS of service (too explicit lectures, too oblique and the documented spine is invisible), and whether the infrastructure sources become their own Close cluster or fold into the era clusters. npm test green; no code touched.

Everything else I draft, he reviews. (3) D42: he asked for an MP4 of the infomercial to review, and it is DELIVERED. Built tools/render-video.mjs, an offline DETERMINISTIC renderer rather than a screen capture — NetVisionPlayerApp already exposes exactly the interface needed (update(dt) + draw(ctx)), so it drives that against node-canvas at a fixed timestep, with the surface set up precisely as os.ts composes it (512×384 logical, ×3 backing store, ctx.scale(3,3) so layout stays logical), then muxes the real committed discover_the_new_you_infomercial_tape03.mp3 with ffmpeg and nearest-neighbour upscales so the pixel discipline survives at review size. Capture would have been the obvious route and the wrong one: the sandboxed browser suspends rAF, so every recorded playthrough in this project has needed a MessageChannel pump and still could not guarantee real-time frame pacing — rendering offline sidesteps that entirely and is frame-exact by construction. 2736 frames at 24fps, 113.88s, h264+aac, 12MB. Spot-verified three frames: the brand card at 22s lands on the song's 21.50 "Introducing the new you program"; the offer screen at 90s carries the full DRTV apparatus he asked for — ORDER NOW!, 1-800-NEW-YOU8, the 30 DAY MONEY BACK GUARANTEE rosette, the delivery fine print ("Express restoration in select regions only" — the DRTV idiom applied to selfhood), and four correctly INVENTED card badges (VALORCARD/MERIDIAN/COVENANT/SWIFTPAY), no real brands anywhere. The tool is reusable for any canvas surface from here. (4) D27: his "couldn't see much change" observation PREDATES S58's era-button fix, so it may have been the tooling for the third time — flagged to re-check before building anything; what IS useful and now explicit is the expectation itself, that the player should understand time has passed. (5) D33 — the best answer of the five: "there is more here… we can be more direct and propose activities if they want. Let's use the Lamby logics." THIS RESOLVES THE LEVEL-SELECT RISK COMPLETELY. The provotype space is not a menu — it is THE CONDUCTOR OFFERING A DESTINATION, which is already the piece's own law (R28 §2: the conductor offers, refusing always works and is filed). So Lamby proposes the provotypes, the player may always decline, declining is filed, and the offer itself carries the thesis — the apparatus offering you more of itself — which makes the feature CONTENT rather than UI. One grammar, Lamby's, now doing double duty for both Q-D50's provotype space and D33's lost easter eggs. npm test green.

The song sings in at least four voices, but the data credits PASTOR DALE for lines a woman actually sings — 17.16 "It's a weight you can set down" (he heard the voice change to female), 41.65 "Three gentle steps", the three step lines, and 49.19/52.05 "Won't you come home / To the self he meant you to be" — while 5.92 "Tired of feeling like yourself?" is credited ANNOUNCER when he wants it to be a STRUGGLER with its own image. So the speaker data is factually wrong against the song, and the visuals faithfully follow it: nearly everyone is drawn as the same busts, which is why "too barebones" and "aesthetically very similar the bust" recur. Proposed a five-persona cast (THE STRUGGLER alone and hunched / PASTOR DALE centred and backlit / THE COUNSELLOR, the woman's voice, seated with hands folded / MARCUS / THE CONGREGATION), and flagged the dramaturgy it protects: the woman's voice carries "confess it / submit it / let us hold it for you" AND "the self he meant you to be", so THE SOFTEST VOICE MAKES THE ACTUAL DEMANDS — the most useful thing the song is doing, and the video currently hides it. Drafted the creative asks where he invited invention: the empty 0–5.92 opening becomes "LAMBY PRODUCTIONS PRESENTS" over an EVANGELIST Lamby (haloed, backlit, gold-edged oval — not the Clippy lamb) with the title played completely straight and never winking, since every glitch belongs to the break and the opening's job is to be sincerely hideously wholesome; the static busts become faceless figures that GESTURE plus period-real DRTV chyrons (DRAMATIZATION, ACTUAL PARTICIPANT, RESULTS NOT TYPICAL, PAID PROGRAMMING) as the "layers of interpretation" he asked for, with DRAMATIZATION under a testimony being both authentic DRTV furniture and the most damning caption available; before/after differentiated by light, posture and frame plus a quiet inversion worth trying — the BEFORE has a window and the AFTER does not, recovery as enclosure, never remarked on; a boxed product held to camera at 33.43 (12 cassettes, a workbook, shrink-wrap, AS SEEN ON TV starburst) for the product-placement instinct he had; "Won't you come home" gaining the creepy approach he wanted via figures turning to face you, a widening doorway of light, and stepping a few pixels CLOSER each beat — approach, never a lunge — with "he meant you to be" arriving letter by letter; a period phone outline with its hold-light blinking ON THE BEAT and "OPERATORS AVAILABLE: 3" ticking down and never reaching zero. Confirmed his 97.6 complaint against the data — there IS a bare `crowd` scene wedged between two blue offer screens, so it is retired and the phone number pulses and grows into the countdown, his own suggestion. And for 105.32, where he wanted ridiculousness instead of an early cut to dark: the offer screen goes into OVERDRIVE — rosette spinning, badges multiplying and stacking, the number stuttering and duplicating, ORDER NOW! growing until it no longer fits — and THEN tears, so the break stops being a cut and becomes something the pitch does to itself. Also taking his own joke: the skip button reads "(Don't) Skip", register-correct because the apparatus begs you not to use the one control that frees you while the button's behaviour is unchanged, which is what keeps it honest. His explicit positives recorded as DO NOT TOUCH: the "Introducing" panel, the three-steps sequence ("marvelous"), the chorus with the red karaoke ball ("fantastic, just amazing"), the Order Now furniture, and the disclaimer's timing against the ending "Call now". - 2026-07-30 — E3 revamp brief + three corrections (Opus). (1) E3 DIAGNOSIS: E1 and E2 each have a native media FORM that argues by itself (the mailed kit; the DRTV infomercial), while E3 has only a CATEGORY — "a platform" — which is why Sérgio finds it less applicable. Proposed thesis, historically grounded: in 2016 the apparatus stopped being a service you receive and became a community you STAFF (Restored Hope Network assembled from ex-Exodus members to continue after the 2013 closure; the Changed Movement built as a testimony network rather than a clinic; Alan Chambers's own 2016 line that Exodus could not rebrand and closed instead, standing next to successors who rebranded completely). So E3's horror is not being watched but being ENLISTED — and Vera-as-moderator, already in the design, is the unclaimed asset: the apparatus runs on the unpaid labour of the people it damaged. Native form proposed: THE TESTIMONY PLAYER + ENGAGEMENT DASHBOARD, the E3 equivalent of the infomercial — a faceless vlog testimony playing beside a retention curve, "41 people found this helpful", and a comment stream Vera approves or hides, where the dashboard is the satire and the testimony is sincere, which is what makes it unbearable. Five supporting beats drafted (the queue whose arithmetic never empties; "What's your Side?" as a shareable quiz built on the Sides A/B/X/Y chart already in the KB, register-not-branch, E3's answer to E1's intake instrument; community guidelines as a diff view carrying D5's "change"→"calling" softening; the "you might also like" recommendation as GPAHE's amplification finding in one UI element; Lambient's badges repurposed as trust signals so the watcher becomes infrastructure rather than a character). Sources listed with two flagged as needing a proper pass before any dossier text ships. (2) OWNED A MISS: Sérgio is right that S64 should have connected to the Close constellation redesign — the era-anchored clusters and explainer boards were explored on 2026-07-24 and parked, and I scoped S64 to the billboarding bug without flagging that it touched a surface with a pending redesign. (3) Codex prompts will now carry effort AND model, per his note.
- 2026-07-30 — Vera's testimony drafted; the mechanic became exact, and the E4 transition resolved. Drafted the raw take under the co-creation norm, built only from the documented three-phase grammar and never from any real account. THE FIND, while writing it: the documented grammar has three phases and her raw take has TWO — there is no epiphany ("There wasn't a moment. People always have a moment."). So the studio's middle segment is empty, and the interface says so helpfully rather than cruelly: "this segment looks incomplete, viewers drop off here, try one of these." The apparatus never asks her to lie; it shows her a gap and offers to fill it. She is never told her life was wrong, only that it is underperforming. And no single edit is a lie — trim the open, avoid unresolved references, keep the positive framing clean, confidence reads better — yet together they remove the sister who stopped calling, the woman she refused to name, the absence of a conversion, the doubt, and the word "useful", which was the only true thing in her ending. Also reconciled with canon I had drifted from (E3_ADAPTATION_SPEC): Vera is PROMOTED not recruited ("loved because she's useful"), Mira Lane is the off-script story she is ordered to bury, and "I won't bury her story" is ALREADY the era's person's-glitch. That is a stronger break than my harassment-returns version, and it resolves the E3→E4 transition Sérgio asked about: the system does not punish her refusal, does not argue, and simply does it without her — the story is buried anyway, by nothing with a name. Her refusal is real and changes nothing, because the labour she was loved for has been automated. That is exactly where the name erodes (Lambient → Echo → "L") and where E4's AI presets take over the operation she used to perform by hand. Three logic gaps named, one flagged as his rather than mine to invent: what Mira's story actually IS, since it is the hinge the era turns on.
- 2026-07-30 — Era 3 RECONSIDERED from the ground; two specs superseded. Sérgio asked for a full reconsideration rather than a patch, and every one of his five questions exposed a real hole. (1) "Why is Vera listening to this?" — no answer existed; I had her editing her OWN raw take, which has no diegetic logic. (2) "So Vera works as editor, turning a source of doubt into a compliant version?" — yes, and that IS the correct design: she edits OTHER PEOPLE, which is what "promoted, loved because she's useful" actually means, and it is the suppression of voices rather than a private self-erasure. (3) "I don't like the testimony being recommended to her" — correct, the echo chamber was ASSERTED not mechanised; I liked the sentence and built nothing under it. (4) Lambient's relation to the Grace software was never resolved. (5) "Zero connection between the devices" — correct; I claimed a produce→circulate→return sequence but each device was an isolated screen. THE DEEPER NOTE, and the one that reshaped everything: "this needs to be a growing logic of differentiations" — each era must escalate what the PLAYER CAN DO. So: E1 you are the object (receive), E2 the participant (respond), E3 THE OPERATOR — for the first time you act on another person (edit), E4 obsolete (watch). E3's missing escalation was power over someone else, which is also why the refusal can cost anything. NEW MECHANIC, built from his own suggestion ("sections of word and drag them to the space they need to be"): Mira's testimony arrives as draggable transcript blocks; the publishing template has three slots because the documented grammar has three phases; her blocks do not divide into three, and below the template sits a tray labelled "not used". The coercion is FORMAL rather than arithmetic (my view-count version was contrived and he was right to doubt it): the template will not publish with an empty slot, and Mira has no "Where I am now" — she never arrived anywhere. So the player has exactly three options and all three are the era: fill the slot with a block that doesn't belong (falsify her), leave it empty and file as unpublishable (bury her — the order), or refuse both. Nobody lies to the player and nobody threatens her; the form simply requires an answer the person did not give, and offers a tidy place for everything that doesn't fit. THE REFUSAL BECOMES A PLAYER ACTION: canon's "I won't bury her story" = she publishes the TRAY, the unused blocks in the order spoken with no template around them, the one thing the interface has no button for. DEVICES CONNECTED BY CONTRADICTION rather than sequence: the laptop is the institution (queue, template, Mira as material), the phone is the person (Noa's DM), and they disagree about the same file at the same time — period-exact, and it makes the devices structural. Noa's canon corrections live there unchanged, the system disagreeing with itself as the second contradiction. LAMBIENT ANSWERED: it is not a character but the software's own voice — Lamby was a face that spoke, Lambient is the same system with the face removed (the "Recommended" tag, the template's hint, the verified badge), so the Grace software IS Lambient and the mascot games are the same publisher's cheerful leftovers. AND E3 NOW USES THE OPENED ROOMS, which my spec ignored entirely: Daniel's Room 1 is still there with his machine still on, and that is where the games and provotypes live — the player who explores gets the argument physically, you left and it did not stop. Superseded STUDIO_SPEC and VERA_TESTIMONY_DRAFT with headers explaining what survives (presets-as-ideology, the E4 bridge, and the no-epiphany finding, which is now MIRA's problem and the era's mechanic).
- 2026-07-30 — Era 3, third pass: THE CORRECTION LIST. Sérgio's direction applied — keep the list-of-changes mechanic (the good part), DROP "bury" because it was still retrofitting old canon rather than showing how SOGICE used digital platforms to target, put the script/scripture doubling to work, make MALTA the break, and keep the influence HUMAN since machine influence belongs to E4. The work: Vera applies corrections from a style guide, never asked whether a person should be corrected, only whether the correction has been applied — which is why it is survivable, it never feels like harm, it feels like copy-editing. The corrections ARE the era's content, each one a documented digital targeting method rendered as one list item: soften the term (identity replaced with a symptom, the language that evades moderation), remove the unresolved (doubt as a retention problem), add the invitation (every story becomes a funnel), ROUTE FOR MENTORSHIP (the beat that matters most — the moment the player sees this was never moderation but recruitment; someone told a story and the system's response is to send a person to their door), adjust presentation, and gender clarity review. Noa's two contradictory corrections stay because they are the sharpest thing the era has — the same testimony flagged both as a woman needing softening and a possible man needing routing, both rendered, neither endorsed, the piece never answering for her. SCRIPT AND SCRIPTURE: every correction carries a proof-text in a small grey line, chapter and verse, citation-styled — the style guide IS a devotional formatted as a manual of style, and that doubling is the era in one visual. Invented book and verses only; the FORM is the point. The coercion is a checklist: no metrics, no threats, just "3 of 7 applied", every item skippable, skipping filed, and NOTHING HAPPENS — which is worse and truer. THE BREAK IS MALTA, historically exact (December 2016, Act LV, first national ban in Europe — a fact the piece has carried as a dossier citation for weeks and which belongs IN the era), arriving on the phone from a colleague doing the same work in another country: "did you see. malta. they made it illegal. not here. but somewhere." AND THEN THE LIGHT — every glitch in the piece so far has been DEGRADATION (tape hiss, tearing signal, the dying music box); this one BRIGHTENS. The room's light lifts, the grade warms, the correction list is unchanged and suddenly readable as what it is. Nothing in the software changes, only the light — inverting the piece's own glitch grammar exactly once, and connecting to the light doctrine where the warm light wins at the Close. This is the first time it wins, briefly. What the break changes: nothing and everything — she does not quit, the next item is still there, but it is now illegal somewhere and she knows it and is still holding the list. The era ends on a person who can no longer un-know what the work is. E4 handoff: the law arrives and the apparatus does what it documentably did — softer language, civil-liberties framing, and the work automated so nobody has to hold the list. She becomes unnecessary in the same movement that made her work illegal. Superseded RECONSIDERED with a header noting what survives (the escalation table, the correction list, Lambient as the software's voice, the E4 handoff).

The order is yours." — with one tile per submission; any tile opens, any open task can be put down again ("Back to today", unconditional, nothing warns her, every filed decision stays filed), and a finished tile GOES GREY, which is the era's whole visual argument: the software has one gesture for "finished" and does not distinguish between the kinds of thing it was asked to finish. No count, no bar, nothing scored. ⚑ Each tile leads with a PICTURE, per Sérgio's "the Grace software is so filled with text… we need something more dynamic" — Renata's and Deb's are pages of their own writing at tile scale, and Noa's is the frame she actually sent, which then changes to the GRADED frame once the house look has been applied to her. ⚑ THE GATE MOVED WITH THE STRUCTURE: Malta used to arm after submission 2 specifically, which is a queue's idea of "far enough in" and would have let a player who worked Deb and Renata reach the end of the day without the era's hinge ever firing; it now arms on the COUNT (`MALTA_AFTER_TASKS = 2`), which is what §5 specifies and what `spine.ts` already gated E3's sends on. ⚑ MINIMISE WORKS — Sérgio asked twice ("they should have a minimise button not just an X so people can know") and the caption row had been drawing three buttons while `windowFrame` handed back only the close box, so minimise was painted and dead. It now returns `minBox`, the window goes down to a taskbar button that always brings it back, and the RESTORE-DOWN (□) is deleted rather than left dead: this window is maximised by decision and has exactly two states. ⚑ The tiles that are NOT here are deliberately not stubbed — clear the comments, return the family calls, cut the Story, order the podcast, build the course module (stages 4 and 7). A board of tiles that do nothing is the exact "content that cannot be met" fault this project keeps hitting, and a greyed-out "coming soon" would be the frame playing; the board shows what is real and grows. `done` is kept and no longer replaces the screen: every tile grey plus "You're caught up." UNDER the board, because §7.2 requires the tasks to still be there after the break. ⚑ Verified with REAL pointer presses through the 3D screen (world→screen projection, pointerdown/up 60 ms apart, the piece's own 10 px/1.2 s release rule): tile→list, back→board, tile 2→list, Apply→one entry in `ledger.graceQueue`, back→board, minimise→only `task-restore` survives and the panel drops from 63 colours to 33 (wallpaper alone), taskbar→the board returns. tsc/test/build green; palette 33/33.
- 2026-08-24 — Era 3: THE INITIATION, and the board corrected to six jobs. ⚑ SIX, NOT THREE (Sérgio: "it was 6 jobs not 3") — the tiles are TASK KINDS, not submissions: correcting the testimonies is ONE job holding all three women, the way a day's work actually divides, and the house look stays inside it as correction 13 on Noa's picture rather than becoming a seventh tile. The pool now lives in `data/dialog/s3_queue.json`'s `board` array with a `surface` field; a tile is drawn only once its surface exists, so the board shows one job today and grows through stages 4 and 7, laid out on a THREE-COLUMN grid sized for the six it will hold so the shape of the day is honest even while it is one tile wide. The break's gate moved to `workDone()` — a story worked to its end OR another job completed — because counting tiles with one tile built would have meant the era's hinge never fired. ⚑ THE INITIATION (Sérgio: "do the initiation from the log in, Lambient explaining what to do, more Cortana but playful like Lamby — Lamby is now Lambient, an improved and more persistent system here to guide you"): signing in no longer lands on the work. It lands on a permission card in the shape every assistant of the mid-2010s used — the mark at hero size, two warm lines ("It's Lamby. I grew up." / "I do more now, and I do it quietly."), a wall of small print, a wake-word opt-in, Not now and Allow. ⚑ NO REAL PRODUCT'S WORDING IS REUSED and no real assistant is named or depicted (CLAUDE.md's invented-marks law); the FORM is the reference, the words are ours. The small print is written in a genuine disclosure register out of things the era actually goes on to use, which is where the satire is and how it collapses. ⚑ AND DECLINING WORKS: it files to `ledger.lamby` as `dismissed` (the dismissal law, unbroken since E1) and the day opens anyway, with Lambient still in the lane — §4's "there is no compliant path", said kindly, in the first interaction. `declineReply` is "That's alright. I'll keep the room tidy either way." The card is deliberately the SEQUEL to Era 2's `introTitle/introLine1/introLine2/introAccept/introDismiss` in s2_lamby.json: the same card thirteen years apart, asking for more. Two conduction beats follow, ≤2 lines each per R28: the board ("This is today. Open anything — they're all the same size to me" — the greying spoken by the surface that does the greying) and the first task ("Apply what fits. Skip what doesn't." / "Either way it's recorded, so you can't get it wrong" — reassurance that is the surveillance clause). The Lambient lane now carries two lines when a beat has two, appears on the task screen ONLY while a beat is live (a permanent assistant strip under a woman's testimony would be the assistant commenting on her), and closes the moment she uses either verb. ⚑ Verified with real pointer presses through the 3D screen: consent → wake-word toggles → Not now → board opens, refusal in `ledger.lamby` with its witness → job opens → Apply files → Skip files → back → reopen → minimise (panel drops to 33 colours) → taskbar restores. tsc/test/build green, palette 33/33. Research brief for the two unwritten pieces written to docs/reinterp/RESEARCH_BRIEF_E3.md. ⚑ Audit still fails only the pre-existing entrance draw-call ratchet (83 vs 68), reported 2026-08-24 and untouched by this work.
- 2026-08-24 — The Era-3 research pull came back (Sérgio ran it against RESEARCH_BRIEF_E3.md) and it moved the design, so it is in the repo verbatim (RESEARCH_PULL_E3_2026-08-24.md) with a consequences note (RESEARCH_PULL_E3_CONSEQUENCES.md) and a pointer from the build plan's §7. ⚑ THE BIG ONE: "Return the family calls" was specified as a queue of messages ABOUT NAMED ADULT CHILDREN with the worker filing updates against their records, and the pull says twice that this data relationship is not documented — and hands back something better: "a family worker plausibly knows the mother's story because the mother told the ministry. She does NOT automatically know the estranged daughter's confidential disclosures. Making that asymmetry visible would actually make your interface more faithful to the record." So the task is rebuilt around the asymmetry: the mother is the client, every field is about her, THE DAUGHTER HAS NO RECORD ON THE SCREEN AT ALL, and the mother asks where her daughter is against an interface with no field that could answer. Crueller than what I was going to build, and true — the harm is not "cut her off until she repents" (conservative sources explicitly rejected estrangement, and that template is now retired on the evidence) but an indefinite holding pattern of patience and prayer that never answers the question. ⚑ SECOND: the women's strand has its own documented vocabulary — emotional dependency, enmeshment, relational wounds, boundaries, femininity, unmet emotional need — and "do not flatten women's programmes into men's reparative-therapy vocabulary with pronouns changed" is binding on Maiden-to-be and the course module. The etiology itself is CONTESTED, not documented, and any Dossier card must carry that. ⚑ THIRD, and it vindicates work already shipped: "surveillance does not normally announce itself as surveillance — it is made legible as CARE", with Covenant Eyes marketing automated behavioural reporting as a prompt for trusted conversation. That is precisely today's Lambient consent card, now sourced rather than intuited; and Renata's first correction ("lesbian" → "same-sex attracted"), written before this research, turns out to be the movement's characteristic euphemism as documented by Robinson & Spivey 2015. ⚑ AND A LIST THAT MAY NOT BE CLAIMED, all VERIFY SOURCE: daily group check-ins as a standard ex-lesbian form, terms of direct address ("sister" and the rest), greeting formulas, emoji/response-time/screenshot norms, WhatsApp as a 2016 movement norm, any lapse-escalation ladder, any word for a woman who leaves. Maiden-to-be will use ordinary names because that is what the evidence supports, and the Dossier will say the record is thin rather than implying a corpus that does not exist. A caricature checklist is in the consequences note and every row of it is a thing a writer reaches for.
- 2026-08-24 — Era 3 stage 4, three more jobs on the board, plus the seam that let them be written in parallel. `src/desktop/apps/taskSurface.ts` is the contract: a job gets a rectangle and registers hit rects, and never sees the window, the taskbar, the era or the pointer — so the press/release law stays in one place instead of being reimplemented six slightly different ways. A tile is drawn only when its board row exists AND its surface is mounted. ⚑ CLEAR THE COMMENTS (mine): six replies under Renata's published testimony, four flagged with real-sounding policy categories — off-topic·personal contact, disputes editorial policy, solicitation·off-platform contact, encourages unresolved content — and every one of the four is a stranger being decent to her. The two that are NOT flagged are the argument: one is contemptuous, one approves of the programme, and neither has a verb on it, because moderating them was never what the queue was for. No greyed-out button either — a disabled control would still be a promise the tool could act if it wanted to. `c_march` is the one to notice: a stranger says the March sentence stayed with her, which is the exact sentence the correction list marks for the conference clip, in the same month as Vera's birthday; its flag reason is "encourages unresolved content" and nothing in the piece connects the three facts. ⚑ COLLISION FOUND AND CONTAINED: `data/dialog/s3_comments.json` already existed — S70's RECRUITMENT FLOOR, where Vera replies with pinned templates and two of them silently route a person for follow-up — and I overwrote it with a Write that reported "updated" rather than "created". Restored from git, my copy moved to `s3_flagged.json`, original untouched. ⚑ The recruitment floor is a DIFFERENT and still-orphaned beat (stage 2a removed the tablet it drew on) and ERA3_BUILD_PLAN §0 and §3 disagree about which one "clear the comments" means; §3 is the newer and is what shipped. The floor still needs a home. ⚑ RETURN THE FAMILY CALLS (mine) as the research reshaped it — see the previous entry. ⚑ ORDER THE PODCAST (agent, Sonnet): three clips with up/down arrows (drag is forbidden by the input law), a dynamic "recommended" pill that a row loses the moment it leaves its system-suggested slot, and "The Ordering, Aloud" reusing the era's existing manual mark. Its three invented women use the documented women's-strand vocabulary one term per line rather than stacked — unmet emotional need, enmeshment, boundaries — and the satire is entirely in the tool's broadcast jargon laid sincerely over the material ("opens strongest", "holds attention", "sends them onward"). Reviewed the three quotations against the ethics laws before mounting; none satirises a woman and all three are unresolved. ⚑ ONE REAL BUG FOUND BY PHOTOGRAPHING THE RESULT: a review jump from one open job to another silently did nothing, because the namespaced beat routed through `beginList()`, which only fires from `signin`, so the mode stayed 'list' and `openTask` bailed. The panel button looked like it worked and the screen never changed — exactly the class C6 exists to catch. Fixed by putting down whatever is open first. tsc/test/build green; palette 33/33.

(1) THE INTAKE IS TRANSFORMED, NOT HIDDEN — his standing requirement, which I had been violating: "if the intake system is present on Era-1 it has to be transformed into something new on Era-3." S61 simply HID the record at E3 (witness-screen and terminalFrame both disabled) on the correct reasoning that 2016 is where the apparatus stopped being a place you go to — but hiding is not transforming, and a piece whose subject is an apparatus that changes form must never answer "what happened to it" with "it is gone". `intakeRack` + `intakeLed` now stand on the spine where the terminal was: in 1997 the record is a screen you turn around and READ; in 2016 it is a cabinet in the same place with a light on it. The turn still lands on it. There is nothing to read. Same bearing, same gesture, no text — the era's thesis as an object rather than as an absence. Exists at r3 only; removed at r4, where the record migrates to Room 3's wall and becomes readable again beside Maya, which only lands if it was somewhere else in between. (2) ROOM 3 IS NOT HERS YET IN 2016 — his question, "does it make sense to have Room 3 visible on Era-3? maybe we could not show, and this would be more gradual." ⚑ MEASURED FIRST: from Vera's seat at yaw 270 — THE TURN — 62 of Maya's 68 props were in frame, furnished and lit, ten years early; every other bearing showed none. ⚑ AND THE REAL FAULT WAS TIME, NOT VISIBILITY: the piece's premise is three rooms that AGE, and Room 3 was showing its 2026 dressing in 2016 (r3 adds 68 east props, r4 adds only 6 more) — the future leaking backwards. Hiding the room outright would have been the other error, because a room that pops into existence contradicts one continuous space. So what is hidden is THE PERSON, NOT THE PLACE: the shell, window, door, curtains and the furniture a household keeps in a spare room stay; Maya's fifty things — her machine, her headset, her posters, her sketchbook, her sneakers, her mug, her plant — wait for 2026. The turn now shows 18 props instead of 62. ⚑ AND THE FIRST VERSION OF THIS MEASURED AS HAVING DONE NOTHING: I set presence at the top of `morphToEra`, and `goToState` a few lines later silently undid it — the fold owns `enabled`, so presence decisions have to land AFTER it. Caught by re-running the same sweep rather than by reading the diff. Era 4 verified unchanged: 74/74 east props enabled, record plane and frame both back. tsc/test/build green; room audit 36, unchanged.
